[ASSISTANT]
The design has plateaued solidly at 2,436,508. For one last micro-optimization, let me reconsider: the v6 critical path spends 3 CSA stages after the PP mux. Let me try replacing the final `corr` CSA stage with a trick — XOR-folding corr into the register-bound sum using an explicit 3-bit half-adder only where needed (since corr ≤ 4 affects only bits 0–2, and cE[0]=0 always). This shortens the final stage from 19-bit XOR+majority to ~3 bits of real add logic:


  -> create_file(content="//#############################################################################\n// v18: v6 base with the final corr merge moved OUTSIDE the CSA tree:\n// (sD,cD) are registered directly (corr-free), and the small correction\n// ncorr (0..4) is added to the registered sum word when it leaves the PE.\n// Since the correction is tiny, the extra add on the s_south->south path\n// is only 4 bits wide (bits [2:0]) plus a cheap carry ripple of at most\n// a few bits; the critical w->register path loses one full CSA stage.\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW   = 8,\n    parameter PWW  = 19,\n    parameter ROW0 = 0)\n   (\n    input                  clk,\n    input                  rst,\n    input                  ld,\n    input                  en,\n    input [DW-1:0]         w_north,\n    input [DW-1:0]         a_west,\n    input [PWW-1:0]        s_north,\n    input [PWW-1:0]        c_north,\n    output [DW-1:0]        w_south,\n    output reg [DW-1:0]    a_east,\n    output reg [PWW-1:0]   s_south,\n    output reg [PWW-1:0]   c_south\n    );\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n        w <= {DW{1'b0}};\n      else if (ld)\n        w <= w_north;\n   end\n\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n        a_east <= {DW{1'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n        a_east <= a_west;\n   end\n\n   wire [PWW-1:0] sa = {{(PWW-DW){a_west[DW-1]}}, a_west};\n\n   function automatic [2:0] decf(input [2:0] b);\n      case (b)\n        3'b000:  decf = 3'b000;\n        3'b001:  decf = 3'b100;\n        3'b010:  decf = 3'b100;\n        3'b011:  decf = 3'b101;\n        3'b100:  decf = 3'b111;\n        3'b101:  decf = 3'b110;\n        3'b110:  decf = 3'b110;\n        3'b111:  decf = 3'b000;\n      endcase\n   endfunction\n\n   wire [2:0] dec [0:3];\n   assign dec[0] = decf({w[1], w[0], 1'b0});\n   assign dec[1] = decf({w[3], w[2], w[1]});\n   assign dec[2] = decf({w[5], w[4], w[3]});\n   assign dec[3] = decf({w[7], w[6], w[5]});\n\n   wire [PWW-1:0] pp [0:3];\n   wire           neg [0:3];\n   genvar k;\n   generate\n      for (k = 0; k < 4; k = k + 1) begin : g_pp\n         wire       sel2   = dec[k][0];\n         wire       isneg  = dec[k][1];\n         wire       isnz   = dec[k][2];\n         wire [PWW-1:0] p1 = {sa[PWW-1-(2*k):0], {(2*k){1'b0}}};\n         wire [PWW-1:0] p2 = {sa[PWW-1-(2*k+1):0], {(2*k+1){1'b0}}};\n         wire [PWW-1:0] pm = sel2 ? p2 : p1;\n         assign neg[k] = isneg;\n         assign pp[k]  = isnz ? (isneg ? ~pm : pm) : {(PWW+1){1'b0}};\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   wire [3:0] ncorr = {1'b0, neg[0]} + {1'b0, neg[1]} +\n                      {1'b0, neg[2]} + {1'b0, neg[3]};\n\n   // ---- 3-level CSA tree (corr not inside) -----------------------------\n   wire [PWW-1:0] sA = pp[0] ^ pp[1] ^ pp[2];\n   wire [PWW-1:0] mA = (pp[0]&pp[1]) | (pp[0]&pp[2]) | (pp[1]&pp[2]);\n   wire [PWW-1:0] cA = {mA[PWW-2:0], 1'b0};\n\n   generate\n      if (ROW0) begin : g_r0\n         wire [PWW-1:0] sB = cA ^ sA ^ pp[3];\n         wire [PWW-1:0] mB = (cA&sA) | (cA&pp[3]) | (sA&pp[3]);\n         wire [PWW-1:0] cB = {mB[PWW-2:0], 1'b0};\n         // corr fold: add ncorr (0..4) into the low bits of sB exactly;\n         // propagate the 2-bit carry ripple through bit 2 upward.\n         wire [PWW-1:0] sC = sB + {{(PWW-4){1'b0}}, ncorr};\n         always @(posedge clk) begin\n            if (rst) begin\n               s_south <= {PWW{1'b0}};\n               c_south <= {PWW{1'b0}};\n            end\n            else if (en) begin\n               s_south <= sC;\n               c_south <= cB;\n            end\n         end\n      end\n      else begin : g_acc\n         wire [PWW-1:0] sB = pp[3] ^ s_north ^ c_north;\n         wire [PWW-1:0] mB = (pp[3]&s_north) | (pp[3]&c_north) |\n                             (s_north&c_north);\n         wire [PWW-1:0] cB = {mB[PWW-2:0], 1'b0};\n         wire [PWW-1:0] sC = sA ^ cA ^ sB;\n         wire [PWW-1:0] mC = (sA&cA) | (sA&sB) | (cA&sB);\n         wire [PWW-1:0] cC = {mC[PWW-2:0], 1'b0};\n         wire [PWW-1:0] sD = cB ^ sC ^ cC;\n         wire [PWW-1:0] mD = (cB&sC) | (cB&cC) | (sC&cC);\n         wire [PWW-1:0] cD = {mD[PWW-2:0], 1'b0};\n         wire [PWW-1:0] sE = sD + {{(PWW-4){1'b0}}, ncorr};\n         always @(posedge clk) begin\n            if (rst) begin\n               s_south <= {PWW{1'b0}};\n               c_south <= {PWW{1'b0}};\n            end\n            else if (en) begin\n               s_south <= sE;\n             clk,\n    input                 rst,\n    input                 ld,\n    input                 en,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]      w_top,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]      a_left,\n    output [N*PWW-1:0]    c_bot\n    );\n\n   wire [DW-1:0]  a_h [0:N-1][0:N];\n   wire [PWW-1:0] s_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [PWW-1:0] c_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [DW-1:0]  w_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n   genvar i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west\n         assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge\n         assign s_v[0][j] N; i = i + 1) begin : g_pe_row\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_pe_col\n            tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .PWW(PWW), .ROW0(i == 0 ? 1 : 0)) u_pe\n              (.clk     (clk),\n               .rst     (rst),\n               .ld      (ld),\n               .en      (en),\n               .w_north (w_v[i][j]),\n               .a_west  (a_h[i][j]),\n               .s_north (s_v[i][j]),\n               .c_north (c_v[i][j]),\n               .w_south (w_v[i+1][j]),\n               .a_east  (a_h[i][j+1]),\n               .s_south (s_v[i+1][j]),\n               .c_south (c_v[i+1][j]));\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N    = 8,\n    parameter DW   = 8,\n    parameter ACCW = 32)\n   (\n    input                 clk,\n    input                 rst,\n    input                 w_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]      w_data,\n    input                 a_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]      a_data,\n    output                c_valid,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]   c_data\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\n   localparam PWW = 2*DW + $clog2(N);\n\n   wire ld = w_valid;\n   wire en = ~w_valid;\n\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   genvar i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n         wire [DW-1:0] din =\n               a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n            reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (k = 0; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= {DW{1'b0}};\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  sr[0] <= din;\n                  for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   wire [N*PWW-1:0] c_bot;\n   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .PWW(PWW)) u_array\n     (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot));\n\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew\n         localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n         wire [PWW-1:0] cin = c_bot[j*PWW +: PWW];\n         if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n                   {{(ACCW-PWW){cin[PWW-1]}}, cin};\n         end\n         else begin : g_deskewn\n            reg [PWW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];\n            integer k;\n filename='design_v18.v')

[ASSISTANT]
The final attempt (v18) was significantly worse — the full-width correction adder created a long carry chain. The best design is firmly established. 

**Final summary:**

**Best design: `design_v6.v`** — cost **2,436,508** area_delay_product (delay 515.9ps × area 4722), vs the starting point baseline of 4,624,409 — a **47% reduction**.

Key optimizations that worked:
1. **Carry-save accumulation** (v4): replaced the 32-bit carry-propagate adder in each PE with a carry-free 3:2 compressor, carrying a redundant (sum, carry) pair south; one 19-bit CPA per column at the output. Cut delay from 1004ps→667ps.
2. **Narrowed internal datapath 32→19 bits**: the sum of eight signed 8×8 products is exactly bounded by ±131072, so 19-bit arithmetic is bit-exact; sign-extension only at the 32-bit output pins.
3. **Radix-4 Booth shift-add multiplier** (v6): replaced the synthesized `*` with 4 Booth partial products (d_k·a·4^k, digits from weight triples) muxed from shifted taps of the sign-extended activation, with a +ncorr correction for one's-complemented negative PPs (~x = −x−1). Function-based decode mapped well to cells.
4. **ROW0 specialization**: top-row PEs skip accumulation logic.

Experiments that didn't help (reverted): registered Booth decode (+FF area), separate correction pipeline (v7), one-hot AND-OR PPs (v8), nsa two's-complement PPs (v9/v17), XOR/AND PPs (v14), carry-LSB elimination (v11), and post-CSA correction adder (v18). FF-init instead of reset failed on the netlist sim path. Target delay 500–600ps was the sweet spot; timing is now fully met at target ≥530ps.
